{D176}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_Ar+ya bi ma la kIr ti nir de sha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/_bod skad du/_'phags pa dri ma med par grags pas bstan pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo/_sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dang /_'phags pa nyan thos dang /_rang 
[\[175a.2\]]sangs rgyas 'das pa dang /_da ltar gyi dang /_ma byon pa thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das yangs pa'i grong khyer na a mra srung ba'i tshal na/_dge slong brgyad stong gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang thabs cig tu 
[\[175a.3\]]bzhugs te/_thams cad kyang dgra bcom pa/_zag pa zad pa/_nyon mongs pa med pa/_dbang dang ldan par gyur pa/_sems shin tu rnam par grol ba/_shes rab shin tu rnam par grol ba/_cang shes pa/_glang po chen po/_bya ba byas pa/_byed pa byas pa/_khur bor ba/_bdag 
[\[175a.4\]]gi don rjes su thob pa/_srid par kun tu sbyor ba yongs su zad pa/_yang dag pa'i shes pas sems shin tu rnam par grol ba/_sems kyi dbang thams cad kyi dam pa'i pha rol du son pa sha stag go/_/byang chub sems dpa' sum khri nyis stong dang yang thabs cig ste/_mngon par shes 
[\[175a.5\]]pa mngon par shes pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po mngon par shes pa chen po yongs su sbyangs pa las nges par byung ba/_sangs rgyas kyis byin gyi rlabs kyis byin gyis brlabs pa/_chos kyi grong khyer srung ba/_dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin pa/_seng ge'i sgra chen po sgrogs pa/_
[\[175a.6\]]phyogs bcur sgra shin tu bsgrags pa/_gsol ba ma btab par sems can thams cad kyi dge ba'i bshes gnyen du gyur pa/_dkon mchog gsum gyi rigs rgyun mi 'chad par byed pa/_bdud dang phyir rgol ba bcom pa pha rol gyi rgol ba thams cad kyi zil gyis mi non pa/_dran pa dang /_
[\[175a.7\]]blo gros dang /_rtogs pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_gzungs dang /_spobs pa phun sum tshogs pa/_sgrib pa dang kun nas sdang ba thams cad dang bral ba/_sgrib pa med pa'i rnam par thar pa la gnas pa/_spobs pa rgyun mi 'chad pa sbyin pa dang /_dul ba dang /_mi 'gyur ba dang /_yang 
[\[175b\]]
[\[175b.1\]]dag par sdom pa dang /_tshul khrims dang /_bzod pa dang /_brtson 'grus dang /_bsam gtan dang /_shes rab dang /_thabs la mkhas pa dang /_smon lam dang /_stobs dang /_ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las nges par byung ba/_mi dmigs pa dang mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa dang ldan pa/_phyir mi ldog 
[\[175b.2\]]pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba/_mtshan nyid med pa'i phyag rgyas btab pa/_sems can thams cad kyi dbang po shes pa la mkhas pa/_'khor thams cad kyi zil gyis mi non pa'i mi 'jigs pas rnam par gnon pa/_bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs chen po bsags pa/_mtshan dang 
[\[175b.3\]]dpe byad bzang po thams cad kyis lus shin tu brgyan pa/_gzugs dam pa 'dzin pa/_rgyan dang bral ba/_ri rab kyi rtse mo mtho ba bzhin du snyan pa dang grags pas mngon par 'phags pa/_lhag pa'i bsam pa rdo rje ltar sra bas sangs rgyas dang chos dang dge 'dun la mi phyed pa'i dad pa rnyed pa/_
[\[175b.4\]]chos rin po che'i 'od zer las bdud rtsi'i char shin tu 'bebs pa/_sems can thams cad kyi skad dang /_smra ba dang /_dbyangs kyi yan lag dang /_sgra rnam par dag pa'i dbyangs dang ldan pa/_chos zab mo rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba la 'jug cing mtha' dang /_mtha' med par lta ba'i bag 
[\[175b.5\]]chags kyi mtshams sbyor ba kun gcod pa dang /_seng ge ltar 'jigs pa med cing sgra mngon par sgrogs pa/_chos chen po'i 'brug sgra sgrogs pa/_mtshungs pa dang mi mtshungs pa'i chos las yang dag par 'das pa/_chos rin po che'i shes rab dang bsod nams kyi tshogs yang dag par 
[\[175b.6\]]sgrub pa'i ded dpon chen po drang ba dang /_zhi ba dang /_phra ba dang /_'jam pa dang /_blta dka' ba dang /_rtogs par dka' ba'i chos kyi tshul la mkhas pa/_sems can rnams kyi 'ong ba dang /_'gro ba dang /_sems can gyi bsam pa rtogs pa'i rjes su zhugs pa'i ye shes kyi yul dang ldan 
[\[175b.7\]]pa/_mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i sangs rgyas kyi ye shes la dbang bskur bas dbang bskur ba/_stobs bcu dang /_mi 'jigs pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi ma 'dres pa la lhag pa'i bsam pas zhugs pa/_ngan song gi 'jigs pa dang /_ngan 'gro'i log par ltung ba'i 'jigs pa'i 
[\[176a\]]
[\[176a.1\]]'obs thams cad las rgal bas bsams bzhin du srid pa'i 'gro bar skye ba ston pa/_sman pa'i rgyal po chen po/_sems can thams cad 'dul ba'i cho ga la mkhas pa/_sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i nad thams cad rtogs pa/_ci rigs par chos kyi sman 
[\[176a.2\]]sbyar ba rab tu sbyor ba/_yon tan mtha' yas pa'i 'byung gnas dang ldan pa/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing mtha' yas par yon tan bkod pas legs par brgyan pa mthong ba dang /_thos pa 'bras bu yod pa/_gom pa 'dor ba 'bras bu yod pa/_bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong dpag 
[\[176a.3\]]tu med par yon tan yongs su brjod kyang yon tan gyi chu bo mtha' yas par rtogs pa la 'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' mnyam par lta ba zhes bya dang /_mnyam mi mnyam lta ba dang /_ting nge 'dzin rnam par sprul pa'i rgyal po dang /_chos kyi dbang phyug dang /_chos kyi tog dang /_
[\[176a.4\]]'od kyi tog dang /_'od bkod pa dang /_rin po che bkod pa dang /_bkod pa chen po dang /_spobs pa brtsegs pa dang /_rin po che brtsegs pa dang /_lag na rin po che dang /_lag na phyag rgya rin po che dang /_rtag tu lag brkyang dang /_rtag tu lag bteg dang /_rtag tu gdung dang /_
[\[176a.5\]]rtag tu dga' dgod dbang po dang /_mchog tu dga' ba'i rgyal po dang /_lha'i rgyal po dang /_smon lam la zhugs pas phyin pa dang /_so so yang dag par rig pa rab tu bsgrub pa thob dang /_nam mkha'i mdzod dang /_rin chen sgron ma 'dzin dang /_rin chen dpa' dang /_rin chen 
[\[176a.6\]]dga' ba dang /_rin chen dpal dang /_mig 'phrul can dang /_dra ba can gyi 'od dang /_dmigs pa med pa'i bsam gtan dang /_shes rab brtsegs dang /_rin chen gtong dang /_bdud 'joms dang /_glog gi lha dang /_rnam par 'phrul pa'i rgyal po dang /_mtshan brtsegs 
[\[176a.7\]]yang dag 'das dang /_seng ge'i nga ro mngon par bsgrags pa'i dbyangs dang /_ri'i rtse mo kun tu 'joms pa'i rgyal po dang /_spos kyi glang po che dang /_spos kyi ba glang glang po che dang /_rtag tu 'bad dang /_brtson pa mi 'dor ba dang /_rab kyi blo gros dang /_mdzes par skyes dang /_
[\[176b\]]
[\[176b.1\]]pad mo'i dpal gyi snying po dang /_pad mo bkod pa dang /_spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang phyug dang /_mthu chen thob dang /_tshangs pa'i dra ba can dang /_rin chen gdan dkar can dang /_bdud las rgyal dang /_zhing snyoms brgyan dang /_nor bu rin chen gdugs dang /_gtsug na gser dang /_
[\[176b.2\]]gtsug na nor bu dang /_byams pa dang /_'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang /_de dag la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' sum khri nyis stong ngo /_/gling chen po bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams mya ngan med pa nas tshangs pa ral pa can la sogs pa tshangs pa khri bcom ldan 'das la lta ba dang /_
[\[176b.3\]]phyag 'tshal ba dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba dang /_chos mnyan pa'i phyir lhags pa de dag kyang 'khor de nyid du 'dus so/_/gling chen po bzhi pa so so nas kyang brgya byin khri nyis stong lhags te/_de dag kyang 'khor de nyid du 'dus so/_/de bzhin du gzhan yang dbang che ba/_dbang che bar grags pa'i 
[\[176b.4\]]tshangs pa dang /_brgya byin dang /_'jig rten skyong dang /_lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po rnams kyang 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur to/_/de bzhin du 'khor bzhi po dge slong dang /_dge slong ma dang /_
[\[176b.5\]]dge bsnyen dang /_dge bsnyen ma rnams kyang der lhags so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal gyi snying po'i seng ge'i khri la bzhugs te/_'khor brgya stong du mas yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis bltas nas chos ston to/_/rgya mtsho'i nang nas ri'i rgyal po ri rab mngon par 'phags pa bzhin 
[\[176b.6\]]du 'khor thams cad zil gyis mnan te lam me lhan ne lhang nger dpal gyi snying po'i seng ge'i khri la bzhugs so/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' lid tsa bI gzhon nu dkon mchog 'byung gnas lid tsa bI gzhon nu lnga brgya tsam dang lhan cig tu rin po che sna bdun gyi gdugs thogs nas yangs pa'i grong 
[\[176b.7\]]khyer chen po nas byung ste/_a mra srung ba'i tshal ga la ba dang /_bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste lhags nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/_bcom ldan 'das la lan bdun bskor ba byas nas/_rin po che'i gdugs ji lta bu thogs pa de dag gis bcom ldan 
[\[177a\]]
[\[177a.1\]]'das la mngon par bskyabs te/_mngon par bskyabs nas phyogs gcig 'khod do/_/rin po che'i gdugs de dag phul ma thag tu de'i mod la sangs rgyas kyi mthus rin po che'i gdugs de dag gcig tu gyur te/_rin po che'i gdugs des stong gsum gyi stong chen 
[\[177a.2\]]po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di thams cad khebs par snang ngo /_/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi chu zheng de dag kyang rin po che'i gdugs chen po de nyid kyi nang na snang ngo /_/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di na ri'i rgyal po ri rab gang dag yod 
[\[177a.3\]]pa rnams dang /_gangs ri dang /_ri btang bzung dang /_ri btang bzung chen po dang /_spos kyi ngad ldan dang /_rin po che'i ri dang /_ri nag po dang /_khor yug dang /_khor yug chen po dang /_de dag thams cad kyang rin po che'i gdugs chen po de nyid kyi nang na snang ngo /_/stong 
[\[177a.4\]]gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di na rgya mtsho chen po gang dag yod pa rnams dang /_mtsho dang /_mtshe'u dang /_rdzing dang /_'bab chu dang /_chu bran dang /_lu ma 'bab pa de dag thams cad kyang rin po che'i gdugs chen po de nyid kyi nang na snang ngo /_/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 
[\[177a.5\]]'jig rten gyi khams 'di na nyi ma dang zla ba'i gzhal med khang dang /_skar ma'i gzugs dang /_lha'i gnas dang /_klu'i gnas dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po'i gnas rnams dang /_rgyal po chen po bzhi'i 
[\[177a.6\]]gnas dang /_grong dang /_gror khyer dang /_grong rdal dang /_yul 'khor dang /_rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor ji snyed yod pa de dag thams cad kyang rin po che'i gdugs chen po gcig po de nyid kyi nang na snang ngo /_/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten na sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chos ston pa 
[\[177a.7\]]gang 'byung ba de yang rin po che'i gdugs chen po gcig po de las 'byung ba'i sgrar grag go/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das las de lta bu'i cho 'phrul chen po 'di mthong nas thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de ngo mtshar du gyur te/_tshim zhing mgu nas yi rangs te/_rab tu dga' zhing dga' ba dang /_
[\[177b\]]
[\[177b.1\]]yid bde ba skyes nas de bzhin gshegs pa la phyag 'tshal te/_mig mi 'dzums par lta zhing 'khod do/_/de nas lid tsa bI gzhon nu dkon mchog 'byung gnas kyis bcom ldan 'das las de lta bu'i cho 'phrul chen po 'di mthong nas pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/_bcom ldan 
[\[177b.2\]]'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod do/_/gtsang zhing mdzes pa pad ma'i 'dab ltar yangs pa'i spyan/_/dgongs pa dag cing zhi gnas pha rol phyin mchog brnyes/_/dge ba'i las bsags yon 
[\[177b.3\]]tan rgya mtsho dpag mi lang /_/dge sbyong zhi ba'i lam brten khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/skyes bu khyu mchog 'dren pa'i rdzu 'phrul khyod gzigs shig_/bde bar gshegs pa'i zhing mchog rab gsal kun kyang gda'/_/khyod kyi chos kyi bka' mchid rgya che 'chi med 'gro/_/de dag thams cad nam mkha' 
[\[177b.4\]]'di yi dkyil na gda'/_/khyod kyi chos kyi rgyal srid mchog 'di chos kyis rgyal/_/rgyal bas 'gro ba rnams la chos kyi nor kyang stsal/_/chos rnams rab tu 'byed mkhas don dam ston mdzad pa/_/chos kyi dbang phyug chos rgyal khyod la mgos phyag 'tshal/_/mchis pa ma lags pa dang ma mchis ma 
[\[177b.5\]]lags dang /_/chos 'di thams cad rgyu las brten nas 'byung ba dang /_/'di la bdag med tshor ba po dang byed med cing /_/dge sdig las ci'ang chud mi za zhes gsung gis ston/_/thub dbang khyod kyis bdud dpung stobs can rab btul nas/_/byang chub mchog rab zhi ba mi 'chi bde ba brnyes/_/
[\[177b.6\]]de la tshor med sems dang yid kyi rgyu ba med/_/mu stegs ngan pa'i tshogs rnams kun gyis de mi rtogs/_/rmad byung chos kyi rgyal po lha mi mngon sum du/_/lan gsum bzlas pa chos kyi 'khor lo rnam mang po/_/shin tu zhi ba rang bzhin rnam dag rab tu bskor/_/de tshe dkon mchog 
[\[177b.7\]]gsum po dag kyang nye bar bstan/_/khyod kyis dkon mchog chos kyis legs par gang btul ba/_/de dag rtog pa ma mchis rtag par rab tu zhi/_/khyod ni skye rga na 'chi mthar 'byin sman pa'i mchog_/yon tan rgya mtsho dpag mi lang la mgos phyag 'tshal/_/bsti stang legs par bgyis 
[\[178a\]]
[\[178a.1\]]pas mi bskyod ri rab bzhin/_/tshul khrims ldan dang tshul khrims 'chal la mtshungs par byams/_/mnyam pa nyid la bzhugs shing thugs kyang nam mkha' bzhin/_/sems can dkon mchog 'di la su zhig mchod mi bgyid/_/thub pa chen po 'khor rnams 'dus pa 'di dag 
[\[178a.2\]]ni/_/khyod kyi zhal du rab tu dad pa'i yid kyis lta/_/thams cad kyis kyang rgyal ba rang gi mdun gnas mthong /_/de ni rgyal ba'i ma 'dres sangs rgyas mtshan lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi gsung gcig rab tu phyung ba yang /_/'khor rnams kyis ni gsung la tha dad rnam par rig_/
[\[178a.3\]]'gro ba dag gis rang gi don bzhin rnam rig pa/_/de ni rgyal ba'i ma 'dres sangs rgyas mtshan lags so/_/gsung gcig rab tu bsgrags pa mdzad pa de yis ni/_/kha cig bag chags bsgos shing kha cig rtogs par byas/_/som nyi 'tshal ba 'dren pas rab tu zhi mdzad de/_/de ni rgyal 
[\[178a.4\]]ba'i ma 'dres sangs rgyas mtshan lags so/_/stobs bcu 'dren pa'i rtsal mnga' khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'jigs pa mi mnga' 'jigs bral khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/ma 'dres pa yi chos rnams nges par rab rtogs pa/_/'gro ba thams cad 'dren pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/kun 
[\[178a.5\]]tu sbyor ba'i bcings pa gcod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/pha rol phyin nas skam la bzhugs la phyag 'tshal lo/_/dub pa'i 'gro ba sgrol ba khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'khor ba'i 'gro bar mi gnas pa la phyag 'tshal lo/_/sems can rnams dang 'gro bar zhugs shing kun 'grogs kyang /_/
[\[178a.6\]]'gro ba thams cad las ni rnam par grol ba'i thugs/_/pad ma dag ni chu skyes chu yis yong mi gos/_/thub pa'i pad mas stong pa nyid ni nges par bsgoms/_/mtshan ma thams cad rnam pa kun tu rab tu bsal/_/khyod ni ci la'ang smon par mdzad pa yong mi mnga'/_/sangs rgyas dag gi 
[\[178a.7\]]mthu chen bsam gyis mi khyab ste/_/nam mkha' lta bur mi gnas pa la bdag phyag 'tshal/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la lid tsa bI gzhon nu dkon mchog 'byung gnas kyis tshigs su bcad pa de dag gis mngon par bstod nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 
[\[178b\]]
[\[178b.1\]]'das lid tsa bI gzhon nu lnga brgya tsam po 'di dag thams cad kyang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par zhugs te/_'di dag kyang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa gang lags zhes sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa zhu na/_bcom ldan 
[\[178b.2\]]'das de bzhin gshegs pas byang chub sems dpa' de dag la sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa legs par bshad du gsol/_de skad ces gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis lid tsa bI gzhon nu dkon mchog 'byung gnas la legs so zhes bya ba byin te/_legs so legs so/_/gzhon 
[\[178b.3\]]nu khyod gang sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa las brtsams te de bzhin gshegs pa la 'dri ba legs so/_/de'i phyir gzhon nu khyod legs par rab tu nyon la yid la zung shig dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa las brtsams te ngas khyod la bshad do/_/
[\[178b.4\]]bcom ldan 'das legs so zhes gsol nas lid tsa bI gzhon nu dkon mchog 'byung gnas dang /_lid tsa bI gzhon nu lnga brgya tsam po de dag bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar nyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis de dag la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/rigs kyi bu sems can gyi zhing ni byang 
[\[178b.5\]]chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ngo /_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_byang chub sems dpa' ji tsam du sems can rnams la rgyas par byed pa de tsam du sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su 'dzin to/_/sems can rnams ji lta bus 'dul bar 'gyur ba de lta bu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su 'dzin to/_/sangs 
[\[178b.6\]]rgyas kyi zhing la 'jug pa ji lta bus sems can rnams sangs rgyas kyi ye shes la 'jug par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su 'dzin to/_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing la 'jug pa ji lta bus sems can rnams 'phags pa lta bus dbang po skye bar 'gyur ba de lta bus sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su 'dzin to/_/
[\[178b.7\]]de ci'i phyir zhe na/_rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing ni sems can gyi don las byung ba'i phyir ro/_/dkon mchog 'byung gnas 'di lta ste dper na nam mkha' la ji lta bur bya ba 'dod pa de bzhin du byed mod kyi nam mkha' la ni byar mi rung zhing brgyan du yang mi rung ba de bzhin du/_
[\[179a\]]
[\[179a.1\]]dkon mchog 'byung gnas chos thams cad nam mkha' dang mtshungs par shes nas byang chub sems dpa' sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi zhing ji lta bur bya bar 'dod pa de lta bur sangs rgyas kyi zhing byed mod kyi/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing nam mkhar ni byar mi rung 
[\[179a.2\]]zhing brgyan du mi rung ngo /_/dkon mchog 'byung gnas 'on kyang bsam pa'i zhing ni byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ste/_de byang chub thob pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing du sems can g.yo med pa dang /_sgyu med pa rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/_/rigs kyi bu lhag pa'i bsam pa'i zhing ni byang chub 
[\[179a.3\]]sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ste/_de byang chub thob pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing du dge ba'i rtsa ba dang tshogs thams cad bsags pa'i sems can rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/_/sbyor ba'i zhing ni byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ste/_de byang chub thob pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing du dge ba'i chos 
[\[179a.4\]]thams cad la gnas pa'i sems can rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa'i sems bskyed pa rgya chen po byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ste/_de byang chub thob pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing der theg pa chen po la yang dag par zhugs pa'i sems can rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/_/sbyin pa'i 
[\[179a.5\]]zhing ni byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ste/_de byang chub thob pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing der bdog pa thams cad yongs su gtong ba'i sems can rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/_/tshul khrims kyi zhing ni byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ste/_de byang chub thob pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing der bsam pa 
[\[179a.6\]]thams cad dang ldan zhing dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam yongs su srung ba'i sems can rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/_/bzod pa'i zhing ni byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ste/_de byang chub thob pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing der mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis brgyan cing bzod pa dang /_dul ba dang zhi gnas 
[\[179a.7\]]dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i sems can rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/_/brtson 'grus kyi zhing ni byang chub sems dpa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ste/_de byang chub thob pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing der dge ba'i chos thams cad la brtson 'grus brtsams pa'i sems can rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/_/bsam gtan gyi

[\[198a.1\]]tsa bI dri ma med par grags pa'i nad 'drir song shig_/'jam dpal gyis kyang gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das lid tsa bI dri ma med par grags pa ni bsnyen par dka' zhing zab mo'i tshul la spobs pa rab tu zhugs pa/_tshig snrel zhi dang rdzogs pa'i tshig bsgrub pa 
[\[198a.2\]]la mkhas pa/_spobs pa rgyun mi 'chad pa/_sems can thams cad la khong khro ba ma mchis pa'i blo dang ldan pa/_byang chub sems dpa'i bgyi ba thams cad tshar phyin pa/_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang /_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang ba'i gnas la shin tu zhugs 
[\[198a.3\]]pa/_bdud thams cad kyi gnas rnam par bzlog pa la mkhas pa/_mngon par shes pa chen pos rnam par rtsen pa/_thabs dang shes rab las nges par 'byung ba/_chos kyi dbyings su gnyis su ma mchis shing dbyer ma mchis pa'i spyod yul gyi dam pa'i mchog thob pa chos kyi 
[\[198a.4\]]dbyings bkod pa gcig pa rnam pa mtha' yas pa'i bkod par chos ston pa la mkhas pa/_sems can thams cad kyi dbang po bkod pa 'thob par bgyid pa 'tshal ba la mkhas pa/_thabs mkhas pa rtogs par khong du chud pa/_dris pa gtan la 'bebs pa thob pa ste/_de ni go che 
[\[198a.5\]]chung ngus mgu bar mi nus kyi/_'on kyang sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis der mchis te yang dag pa ji lta ba dang /_mthu ji lta ba bzhin du bshad par 'tshal lo/_/de nas 'khor de na byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang /_nyan thos chen po de dag dang /_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_
[\[198a.6\]]'jig rten skyong ba de dag dang /_lha'i bu de dag dang /_lha'i bu mo de dag 'di snyam du sems so/_/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang /_skyes bu dam pa de gnyis smra ba las/_der ni chos bsgrags pa'i gtam chen po gdon mi za bar 'byung ngo snyam ste de nas byang chub sems dpa' brgya 
[\[198a.7\]]stong dang /_nyan thos lnga brgya tsam dang /_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten skyong ba shin tu mang ba dang /_lha'i bu brgya stong phrag shin tu mang po rnams chos mnyan pa'i phyir 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa'i phyi bzhin dong ngo /_/de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa 
[\[198b\]]
[\[198b.1\]]byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang /_nyan thos chen po de dag dang /_brgya byin dang /_tshangs pa dang /_'jig rten skyong ba de dag dang /_lha'i bu de dag thams cad kyis yongs su bskor cing mdun du bdar te/_yangs pa'i grong khyer chen por zhugs so/_/de nas lid tsa bI dri ma med 
[\[198b.2\]]par grags pa 'di snyam du sems te/_'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa 'khor mang po dang 'ong gis bdag gi khyim 'di stong par byin gyis brlab po snyam ste/_des khyim de stong par byin gyis brlabs nas de na sgo ba'ang med do/_/khri gang la dri ma med par grags pa na ba nyal zhing 
[\[198b.3\]]'dug pa'i khri gcig po de ma gtogs par de na khri 'am khri'u 'am/_stan gang yang mi snang ngo /_/de nas 'jam dpal 'khor dang bcas pa dri ma med par grags pa'i gnas ga la ba der dong ste lhags nas nang du 'ongs pa dang khyim de stong par mthong ste/_de na sgo ba'ang med/_dri ma med 
[\[198b.4\]]par grags pa nyal zhing 'dug pa'i khri gcig pu de las gzhan khri 'am khri'u 'am/_stan ma mthong ngo /_/de nas lid tsa bI dri ma med par grags pas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa mthong ste/_mthong nas 'di skad ces smras so/_/'jam dpal legs par 'ongs so/_/'jam 
[\[198b.5\]]dpal shin tu legs par 'ongs so/_/sngon ma 'ongs/_ma mthong /_ma thos pa mthong ngo /_/'jam dpal gyis smras pa khyim bdag ji skad zer ba de bzhin te/_gang 'ongs pa de ni phyis mi 'ong ngo /_/gang song ba de yang phyis mi 'gro'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_ma 'ongs pa la 'ong bar shes 
[\[198b.6\]]pa yang med cing song ba la yang 'gro bar shes pa med pa la/_gang mthong ba de ni phyir yang blta bar bya ba ma yin pa'i phyir ro/_/skyes bu dam pa khyod bzod dam/_'tsho 'am/_khyod kyi khams rnams mi 'khrug gam/_khyod kyi nad de 'byung ngam/_mi skye 'am/_bcom ldan 'das 
[\[198b.7\]]kyang khyod la gnod pa chung ngam/_nyam nga ba nyung ngam/_na ba nyung ngam/_bskyod pa yang ngam/_'tsho ba dang /_stobs dang /_bde ba dang /_kha na ma tho ba dang /_bde bar reg pa la gnas sam zhes gsung ngo /_/khyim bdag khyod kyi nad 'di ci las byung /_byung nas ci srid lon/_
[\[199a\]]
[\[199a.1\]]ji ltar gnas/_ci tsam na zhi bar 'gyur/_dri ma med par grags pas smras pa/_'jam dpal ma rig pa dang /_srid pa'i sred pa ji srid par kho bo'i nad 'di 'ang de srid do/_/sems can thams cad kyi nad ji srid par kho bo'i nad kyang de srid do/_/nam sems 
[\[199a.2\]]can thams cad nad dang bral bar gyur pa de na kho bo'i nad kyang 'byung ngo /_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'jam dpal byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor ba'i gnas ni sems can te/_nad ni 'khor ba la gnas so/_/nam sems can thams cad nad dang bral bar gyur pa de na byang chub sems 
[\[199a.3\]]dpa' yang nad med par 'gyur ro/_/'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na tshong dpon gyi bu gcig pa na bar gyur na de na ba'i phyir pha ma gnyis kyang na bar 'gyur ro/_/ji srid du bu gcig pa de nad med par ma gyur pa de srid du pha ma gnyis kyang sdug bsngal bar 'gyur te 'jam dpal de bzhin 
[\[199a.4\]]du byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad la bu gcig pa bzhin du sdug pa yin te/_sems can thams cad na bas de yang na bar 'gyur/_sems can la nad med na de yang na ba med do/_/gang 'jam dpal khyod nad 'di ci las byung zhes zer ba yang byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[199a.5\]]rnams kyi nad ni snying rje chen po las byung ngo /_/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/_khyim bdag khyod kyi khyim 'di stong na khyod la g.yog 'ga' yang med dam/_smras pa/_'jam dpal sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyang stong ngo /_/smras pa cis stong /_smras pa/_stong 
[\[199a.6\]]pa nyid kyis stong ngo /_/smras pa/_stong pa nyid la stong pa ci zhig yod/_smras pa/_kun tu rtog pa ni/_stong pa nyid kyis stong pa'o/_/smras pa/_stong pa nyid kun tu brtag par nus sam/_smras pa/_yongs su rtog pa de yang stong pa ste/_stong pa nyid ni stong pa nyid la mi 
[\[199a.7\]]rtog go/_/smras pa/_khyim bdag stong pa nyid gang du btsal/_smras pa/_'jam dpal stong pa nyid ni lta bar gyur pa drug cu rtsa gnyis las btsal lo/_/smras pa/_lta bar gyur pa drug cu rtsa gnyis gang las btsal/_smras pa/_de de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par grol 
[\[199b\]]
[\[199b.1\]]ba las btsal lo/_/smras pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par grol ba'ang gang las btsal/_smras pa/_sems can thams cad kyi sems dang po spyod pa las btsal lo/_/'jam dpal gang khyod la g.yog kyang med dam zhes zer ba ni bdud thams cad dang /_phas kyi 
[\[199b.2\]]rgol ba thams cad ni kho bo'i g.yog yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_bdud rnams ni 'khor ba'i bsngags pa smra ba ste/_'khor ba ni byang chub sems dpa'i g.yog go/_/phas kyi rgol ba rnams ni lta bar gyur pa rnams kyi bsngags pa smra ba ste/_byang chub sems dpa' ni 
[\[199b.3\]]lta bar gyur pa thams cad las mi g.yo'o/_/de bas na bdud thams cad dang phas kyi rgol ba thams cad ni kho bo'i g.yog go/_/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/_khyim bdag khyod kyi nad ci 'dra/_smras pa/_gzugs med cing bstan du med do/_/smras pa/_nad de 
[\[199b.4\]]lus dang ldan nam/_'on te sems dang ldan/_smras pa/_lus dben pas na lus dang ldan pa yang ma yin/_sems sgyu ma'i chos nyid pas na sems dang yang mi ldan no/_/smras pa/_khyim bdag 'di lta ste/_sa'i khams dang /_chu'i khams dang /_me'i 
[\[199b.5\]]khams dang /_rlung gi khams dang /_khams 'di bzhi las khams gang gnod par 'gyur/_smras pa/_'jam dpal sems can thams cad kyi nad khams de lta bu gang yin pa des kho bo yang na'o/_/'jam dpal ji ltar na byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa' na 
[\[199b.6\]]ba yang dag par dga' bar bya/_'jam dpal gyis smras pa/_lus kyi mi rtag pa ste/_yid byung zhing 'dod chags dang bral bas ni ma yin no/_/lus sdug bsngal ba ste/_mya ngan las 'das pa la dga' bas ni ma yin no/_/lus bdag med pa ste/_'on kyang sems 
[\[199b.7\]]can smin par bya bas so/_/lus zhi ba nyid kyis te/_shin tu zhi bas ni ma yin no/_/nyes par spyod pa thams cad bstan pa ste/_'pho bas ni ma yin no/_/bdag na bas sems can gzhan na ba la snying rje ba dang /_sngon gyi mtha'i mu'i sdug bsngal rjes su dran pa dang /_sems 
[\[200a\]]
[\[200a.1\]]can gyi don bya ba rjes su dran pa dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba mngon du bya ba dang /_gzod ma nas rnam par dag pa dang /_sred pa med pa dang /_rtag tu brtson 'grus brtsams pas nad thams cad med par byed pa'i sman pa'i rgyal por 'gyur ro zhes de ltar byang chub sems dpas 
[\[200a.2\]]byang chub sems dpa' na ba yang dag par dga' bar bya'o/_/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/_rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' na bas ji ltar rang gi sems nges par bsam par bya/_dri ma med par grags pas smras pa/_'jam dpal byang chub sems dpa' na bas ni bdag 
[\[200a.3\]]gi sems la 'di ltar nges par bsam par bya'o/_/nad ni sngon gyi mtha' nas yod pa ma yin pa phyin ci log gi las kun nas ldang ba las byung ngo /_/yang dag pa ma yin pa kun tu rtog pa'i nyon mongs pa las byung ste/_don dam par ni 'di la gang gi na ba zhes bya ba'i chos de gang 
[\[200a.4\]]yang mi dmigs so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'byung ba chen po bzhi las gyur pa'i lus 'di khams 'di dag la bdag po gang yang med de bskyed pa'ang med do/_/lus 'di la bdag med de/_bdag tu mngon par zhen pa ma gtogs par 'di la don dam par na gang nad ces bya 
[\[200a.5\]]ba de mi dmigs te/_de bas na bdag ni mngon par zhen pa med cing /_nad kyi rtsa ba kun shes pa la gnas par bya'o/_/zhes des bdag tu 'du shes pa rgyun bcad de chos kyi 'du shes bskyed par bya'o/_/lus 'di ni chos mang po 'dus pa ste skye ba na yang chos kho na skye/_'gag 
[\[200a.6\]]pa na yang chos kho na 'gag ste/_chos de dag ni phan tshun du mi tshor mi shes te/_chos de dag skye ba na yang 'di snyam du bdag skye'o snyam du mi sems so/_/'gag pa na yang 'di snyam du bdag 'gag go snyam du mi sems so/_/des chos kyi 'du shes kun shes par bya ba'i phyir sems 
[\[200a.7\]]bskyed par bya ste/_gang bdag gis 'di ltar chos su 'du shes pa de yang phyin ci log ste/_phyin ci log ni nad chen po'o/_/bdag ni nad dang bral bar bya ste/_nad rab tu spang ba'i phyir brtson par bya'o/_/de la nad spang ba de gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_ngar 'dzin pa dang /_nga yir 
[\[200b\]]
[\[200b.1\]]'dzin pa spang ba'o/_/ngar 'dzin pa dang /_nga yir 'dzin pa spang ba de gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_gnyis dang bral ba'o/_/de la gnyis dang bral ba gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_nang dang phyi rol tu kun tu spyod pa med pa'o/_/de la nang dang phyi rol tu kun tu spyod pa med pa de gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_
[\[200b.2\]]mnyam pa nyid las mi g.yo ba/_rab tu mi g.yo ba/_rnam par mi g.yo ba'o/_/mnyam pa nyid gang zhe na/_bdag mnyam pa nyid nas mya ngan las 'das pa mnyam pa nyid kyi bar du'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_bdag dang mya ngan las 'das pa gnyi ga'ang stong pa'i phyir ro/_/
[\[200b.3\]]de gnyi ga cis stong zhe na/_ming du brjod pa de gnyis stong ste/_de dag kyang yongs su ma grub pa'o/_/de ltar mnyam pa nyid mthong ba des nad nyid gzhan ma yin/_stong pa nyid gzhan du mi byed de/_nad nyid stong pa nyid do/_/tshor ba de ni tshor ba med par rig par bya ste/_des tshor ba 
[\[200b.4\]]'gog pa yang mngon sum du mi bya'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi chos yongs su rdzogs pa la tshor ba gnyi ga dor mod kyi ngan 'gror skyes pa'i sems can thams cad la snying rje chen po mi skyed pa'ang ma yin no/_/ji ltar sems can 'di dag la tshul bzhin nges par sems pas nad sel bar 
[\[200b.5\]]'gyur ba de ltar bya'o/_/'di dag la chos gang yang nye bar sbyar bar yang mi bya/_bsal bar yang mi bya ste/_de gzhi gang las nad byung ba de yongs su shes par bya ba'i phyir de dag la chos bshad par bya'o/_/gzhi de gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_lhag par dmigs pa ni gzhi'o/_/
[\[200b.6\]]lhag par dmigs pa'i gzhi la ji tsam du dmigs pa de tsam du nad kyi gzhi'o/_/ci la lhag par dmigs she na/_khams gsum la lhag par dmigs so/_/lhag par dmigs pa'i gzhi yongs su shes pa gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_dmigs pa med cing mi dmigs 
[\[200b.7\]]pa'o/_/gang mi dmigs pa de ni lhag par mi dmigs pa'o/_/ci mi dmigs she na/_'di lta ste/_bdag tu lta ba dang /_pha rol tu lta ba dang /_lta ba gnyis su mi dmigs te/_des na mi dmigs pa zhes bya'o/_/'jam dpal de ltar byang chub sems dpa' na bas rga ba dang /_
[\[201a\]]
[\[201a.1\]]na ba dang /_'chi ba dang /_skye ba spang ba'i phyir bdag gi sems la nges par bsam par bya'o/_/'jam dpal byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi nad ni de lta bu yin te/_gal te de lta ma yin du zin na/_'bad pa don med par 'gyur ro/_/'di lta ste/_dper bya na phyir rgol ba 
[\[201a.2\]]bcom pas dpa' bo zhes bya ba de bzhin du rga ba dang /_na ba dang /_'chi ba'i sdug bsngal zhi bar byed pas byang chub sems dpa' zhes bya'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' na ba des 'di snyam du ji ltar bdag gi nad yang dag pa ma yin zhing yod pa ma yin pa de bzhin du sems 
[\[201a.3\]]can thams cad kyi nad kyang yang dag pa ma yin zhing yod pa ma yin no zhes brtag par bya ste/_de de ltar rtog pa na phan yon du lta bar mi ltung zhing sems can rnams la snying rje chen po skye ste/_blo bur gyi nyon mongs pa spang ba'i phyir mngon par brtson pa'i snying rje chen po sems 
[\[201a.4\]]can rnams la skye ba ni ma gtogs so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_phan yon du lta bar ltung ba'i snying rje chen pos ni skye ba rnams su byang chub sems dpa' skyo bar 'gyur ro/_/phan yon du lta ba'i kun nas ldang ba dang bral ba'i snying rje chen pos ni byang chub sems dpa' skye ba rnams 
[\[201a.5\]]su skyo bar mi 'gyur ro/_/lta ba kun nas ldang ba kun tu ldang zhing mi skye ste/_de sems kun nas ldang ba med par skye bas de grol bzhin du skye/_de grol bzhin du 'byung ngo /_/grol bzhin du skye zhing grol bzhin du 'byung bas sems can bcings pa rnams bcings pa las grol ba'i 
[\[201a.6\]]chos ston par nus shing mthu yod do/_/'di lta ste/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag nyid bcings pas pha rol bcings pa las 'grol ba de ni gnas med de/_bdag grol bas pha rol bcings pa las 'grol ba de ni gnas yod do zhes gsungs te/_de bas na byang chub sems 
[\[201a.7\]]dpa' grol bar bya'i/_bcings par ni mi bya'o/_/de la bcings pa gang /_grol ba gang zhe na/_thabs med par srid pa'i grol ba yongs su 'dzin pa ni/_byang chub sems dpa'i bcings pa'o/_/thabs kyis srid pa'i 'gro bar 'jug pa ni grol ba'o/_/thabs med 
[\[201b\]]
[\[201b.1\]]pas bsam gtan dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa ro myang ba ni byang chub sems dpa'i bcings pa'o/_/thabs kyis bsam gtan dang /_ting nge 'dzin gyi ro myang ba ni grol ba'o/_/thabs kyis ma zin pa'i shes rab ni bcings pa'o/_/thabs kyis zin 
[\[201b.2\]]pa'i shes rab ni grol ba'o/_/shes rab kyis ma zin pa'i thabs ni bcings pa'o/_/shes rab kyis zin pa'i thabs ni grol ba'o/_/de la thabs kyis ma zin pa'i shes rab bcings pa gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_stong pa nyid dang /_mtshan ma med pa dang /_smon pa med pa la nges 
[\[201b.3\]]par sems shing /_mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan dang /_sems can yongs su smin par bya ba la nges par mi sems pa ni thabs kyis ma zin pa'i shes rab ste bcings pa'o/_/de la thabs kyis zin pa'i shes rab grol ba gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_mtshan 
[\[201b.4\]]dang dpe byad bzang po dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan dang /_sems can yongs su smin par bya ba la sems nges par sems shing stong pa nyid dang /_mtshan ma med pa dang smon pa med pa la 'dris par byed pa 'di ni thabs kyis zin pa'i shes rab ste grol ba'o/_/de la shes rab

[\[201b.6\]]thabs te bcings pa'o/_/de la shes rab kyis zin pa'i thabs grol ba gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_lta ba thams cad dang /_nyon mongs pa kun nas ldang ba dang /_bag la nyal ba dang /_rjes su chags pa dang /_khong khro ba spangs pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba rtsom pa thams cad byang chub 
[\[201b.7\]]tu yongs su bsngos la des kyang mi snyems pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i shes rab kyis zin pa'i thabs te grol ba'o/_/'jam dpal de la byang chub sems dpa' na bas de ltar chos de dag la nges par bsam par bya ste/_gang lus dang sems dang /_nad la mi rtag pa dang /_sdug bsngal 
[\[202a\]]
[\[202a.1\]]ba dang /_stong pa dang /_bdag med par rtog pa de ni de'i shes rab bo/_/gang yang lus kyi nad yongs su spong bas mi skyo ste/_'khor ba'i rgyun mi gcod cing sems can gyi don sbyor ba la rjes su sbyor ba 'di na de'i thabs so/_/gzhan yang gang lus dang /_sems dang /_nad 
[\[202a.2\]]rnams phan tshun du gcig nas gcig tu gsar pa yang ma yin/_rnying pa yang ma yin par rtog pa de ni de'i shes rab bo/_/gang yang lus dang /_sems dang /_nad rnams shin tu zhi ba dang 'gog pa mi skyed pa de ni de'i thabs so/_/'jam dpal de ltar byang chub sems dpa' na bas 
[\[202a.3\]]rang gi sems nges par bsam par bya mod kyi des nges par sems pa dang /_nges par mi sems par mi bya'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_gal te nges par sems pa la gnas na de ni byis pa'i chos so/_/ci ste nges par mi sems pa la gnas na de ni nyan thos kyi chos so/_/de 
[\[202a.4\]]lta bas na byang chub sems dpas nges par sems pa dang /_mi sems pa la gnas par mi bya'o/_/gang de la mi gnas pa ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul te/_gang so so'i skye bo'i spyod yul yang ma yin/_'phags pa'i spyod yul yang ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod 
[\[202a.5\]]yul lo/_/gang 'khor ba'ang spyod yul la nyon mongs pa'ang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang mya ngan las 'das par rtog par yang spyod yul la gtan du yongs su mya ngan las 'das par yang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i 
[\[202a.6\]]spyod yul lo/_/gang bdud bzhi ston pa yang spyod yul la bdud kyi yul thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa yang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes tshol ba yang spyod yul la dus ma yin par ye shes 'thob pa 
[\[202a.7\]]yang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang bden pa bzhi shes pa yang spyod yul la dus ma yin par bden pa rtogs par byed pa yang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang nang du so sor rtog pa yang spyod yul la bsams 
[\[202b\]]
[\[202b.1\]]bzhin du srid par skye bar yongs su 'dzin pa'ang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang mi skye bar so sor rtog pa'ang spyod yul la nges par gyur pa la 'jug pa'ang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba 
[\[202b.2\]]yang spyod yul la lta ba thams cad kyi yul yang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang sems can thams cad kyi 'du 'dzi yang spyod yul la nyon mongs pa'i bag la nyal yang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang dben 
[\[202b.3\]]pa yang spyod yul la lus dang sems zad pa'i gnas kyang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang khams gsum yang spyod yul la chos kyi dbyings dbye bar bya ba'ang spyod yul ma yin pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo/_/gang stong pa nyid kyang spyod yul
